local function __at_check()
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, 2, true, 0.5)
        if typeof(ti) ~= "TweenInfo" or ti.Time ~= 1 or ti.RepeatCount ~= 2 then return 0 end
        if ti.EasingStyle ~= Enum.EasingStyle.Quad or ti.Reverses ~= true then return 0 end
        if math.abs(ti.DelayTime - 0.5) > 1e-6 then return 0 end

        local f = Faces.new(Enum.NormalId.Top, Enum.NormalId.Bottom)
        if typeof(f) ~= "Faces" or f.Top ~= true or f.Bottom ~= true or f.Right ~= false then return 0 end

        local ax = Axes.new(Enum.Axis.X)
        if typeof(ax) ~= "Axes" or ax.X ~= true or ax.Y ~= false or ax.Z ~= false then return 0 end

        local r = Ray.new(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
        if typeof(r) ~= "Ray" or r.Origin ~= Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) then return 0 end
        if math.abs(r.Direction.Unit.Y - 1) > 1e-5 then return 0 end

        local idx = table.find({10, 20, 30}, 20)
        if idx ~= 2 then return 0 end
        local moved = table.move({10, 20, 30}, 1, 3, 1, {})
        if type(moved) ~= "table" or moved[1] ~= 10 or moved[3] ~= 30 then return 0 end
        local packed = table.pack(10, 20, 30)
        if packed.n ~= 3 or packed[1] ~= 10 or packed[3] ~= 30 then return 0 end
        local m = string.match("hello123world", "%d+")
        if m ~= "123" then return 0 end
        local cnt = 0
        for _ in string.gmatch("aaaa", "a") do cnt = cnt + 1 end
        if cnt ~= 4 then return 0 end

        local s = 0
        for i = 1, 50 do s = s + i end
        if s ~= 1275 then return 0 end

        return ti.RepeatCount * 10 + ti.Time + r.Direction.Unit.Y + 75
    end)
    if not ok or type(res) ~= "number" or res == 0 then
        while true do end
    end
    return res
end
local __at_val = __at_check()
if type(__at_val) ~= "number" then while true do end end

local fBflW13NM78EPJ = function(t, k)
    local r = {}
    local kb = {}
    for i = 0, 23 do kb[i+1] = (k + i * 13 + 7) % 256 end
    for i = 1, #t do
        local b = (t[i] * 171) % 256
        b = (b - 37) % 256
        r[i] = string.char(b ~ kb[((i-1) % 24) + 1])
    end
    return table.concat(r)
end
